# Agentic Graphics daily report
Date: 2026-11-20 | Timezone: America/Chicago | As of: 2026-11-23T13:44:41+00:00
browser-based same-day search; documented source coverage
Taxonomy: survey-taxonomy-snapshot-2026-10-15 | Snapshot: bundled_snapshot_not_live_refreshed

## Coverage limits
- Search indexes and public release pages may lag; field-wide completeness is not established.
- One same-day core submission verified; SKILL.PNG same-day revision excluded on domain grounds.
- Three caught-up events belong to November18 Chicago (Inhabitant, SceneAnything, Efficient Multimodal Agents), discovered after that pass was finalized; earlier report is not retrospectively modified.
- Detailed method reading prioritized SIR-Agent and Inhabitant; other retained candidates received bounded abstract/method screening.
- cs.GR recent and cs.CV recent/month second page were inspected selectively; cs.RO rotation included, cs.AI/cs.HC/cs.MM/eess.IV not opened in this pass.
- OpenReview group returned Loading; CVPR2026 proceedings index returned403. SIGGRAPH program page did not establish same-day paper events.
- Ten search batches and 24 attempted primary opens are count caps; retrieval stops when those caps are reached. Actual elapsed time is reported, not padded to six minutes.
- Queries and page reads are grouped by tool invocation in the ledger; a source-family association does not imply every result in that batch came from that family.
- Search snippets and historical daily claims are leads only. Full-paper reproduction, exhaustive listing scans, citation chaining, and independent experimental validation were not performed.
- The tools field remains provisional; the bundled memory definition is internally inconsistent. Unclear cells mean insufficient inspected evidence, not an inferred negative.

## same_day_new (1)

### [SIR-Agent: Skill-Augmented Image Restoration Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27285)
core | High | new_relative_to_snapshot
Application: 2D Graphics; operations: Editing
External skill and revision-rule evolution with frozen model parameters.
Scope: Image restoration agent selects tool skills, diagnoses intermediate degradation, and revises reusable operations with replay validation.
Limits: Selected method sections. Skill evolution is not weight training; general-purpose software integration and memory sharing are not established.

| Taxonomy field | Values | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| task | Unclear |  |
| input_form | Image | Degraded image starts restoration. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1, line 87) |
| output | Rendered Media | Restoration tools yield restored image. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1, line 88) |
| model_type | VLM | MLLM inspects intermediate images. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2, line 92) |
| model_training | Off-the-Shelf | MLLM parameters remain frozen. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), Introduction, line 76) |
| planning | PO-2 | Fixed skill execution/revision stages with model selection and diagnosis. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1–3.3) |
| workflow | Sequential | Selected operations execute in specified order. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1, line 88) |
| update_strategy | Local Patch, Candidate Selection | Compares tools and substitutes selected operation configurations. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2, line 93) |
| verifier | Self, Programmatic | MLLM diagnoses; replay quality metrics gate patches. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2, lines 92–104) |
| feedback_assessment | Score, Diagnosis | Quality differences and weak-operation localization guide revision. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2) |
| feedback_observation | Visual | Intermediate restored images inform diagnosis. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2, line 92) |
| memory_time | Long-Term Memory | Accepted skills and revision rules support later tasks. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1–3.3) |
| memory_reach | Unclear |  |
| action_type | Tool/Function Call | Invokes configured restoration tools. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.1) |
| graphics_software | Unclear |  |
| tools | Unclear |  |
| resources | Examples & Trajectories, Documentation & Rules | Historical restoration traces and revised external rules. ([source](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.27285v1), 3.2–3.3) |

### [Inhabitant: An Agent Framework for Long-Horizon Human-Scene Interaction with Spatial Control and Quantitative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25005)
core | High | new_relative_to_snapshot
Application: Dynamic / Animation and temporal scene, Dynamic / Physical simulation and effects; operations: Generation, Editing
Generates offline human motion sequences through tool-directed spatial controls, simulated trials, measurements, and revision.
Scope: Generates offline human motion sequences through tool-directed spatial controls, simulated trials, measurements, and revision.
Limits: Half-physics simulator uses kinematic body movement and rule-based grasping. Hidden success rules are final evaluation, not agent feedback. Late discovery of November 18 event.
